# Modernization Brief - MagicBox Modernization

## Header
- Objective: Migrate the existing PHP codebase to a modern architecture using Blazor and a new database.
- Domain: software
- Repo: https://github.com/vishakjai/magicbox @ codex-synthetix-ui-v2-claude (59e8835b41a7fee4dfd86a0aaaf871038f3fe2df)
- SIL Versions: SCM scm-v1 / CP cp-v1 / HA ha-v1
- Generated At: 2026-04-24T15:59:00.645245+00:00

## Decision Brief

| Category | Summary |
|---|---|
| Modernization readiness | 75/100 |
| Risk tier | medium |
| Inventory | 1 application(s), 249 controllers, 785 routes, 1684 templates, 3 dependencies |
| Lines of code scanned | 565185 total LOC across 3827 files |
| Data touchpoints |  |
| Headline | Strangler Fig Pattern recommended. |

### Recommended strategy
- Strangler Fig Pattern: Allows gradual migration while maintaining existing functionalities.
- PH0 Route, session, and data baseline: Capture route inventory, session behavior, and SQL touchpoints.
- PH1 Controller and service modernization: Migrate controller workflows and extract reusable service boundaries.
- PH2 Hardening and release evidence: Finalize parity evidence, quality gates, and release documentation.

### Decisions Required (Blocking)
- DEC-PHP-ARCH-001: Target runtime and application architecture for the PHP modernization
  - Recommendation: Use a TypeScript modular monolith first unless there is a clear reason to decompose immediately.
- DEC-PHP-DB-001: Database contract strategy during PHP migration
  - Recommendation: Preserve contracts initially and modernize behind a compatibility layer.
- DEC-PHP-ASYNC-003: Background job or queue listener migration strategy requires confirmation
  - Recommendation: Preserve asynchronous behavior explicitly and inventory listeners/cron jobs before implementation.
- DEC-PHP-SESSION-004: Session and authentication migration approach must be confirmed
  - Recommendation: Model current session/auth behavior first, then move to a clearer target design with explicit compatibility rules.
- DEC-IAM-001: Confirm identity/access model (role model, multi-user assumptions, and credential handling).
  - Recommendation: Define target role model and credential policy before implementation.
- Q-001: Are there existing operational constraints or integration dependencies not listed?
  - Recommendation: Resolve with product/business owner before implementation commitment.
- Q-002: What are target latency, throughput, and availability SLOs?
  - Recommendation: Resolve with product/business owner before implementation commitment.
- DEC-BDD-001: Ground BDD acceptance criteria in real legacy golden flows before Build planning.
  - Recommendation: Regenerate BDD from validated golden flows, route evidence, and SQL touchpoints before Build starts.

### Decisions Required (Non-blocking)
- DEC-OBS-001: Logging and observability stack for migrated runtime
